D_DECODE = {
    "D0":  {1: ("info", "I-000: PLC 通電完了", "システム起動確認")},
    "D1":  {1: ("info", "I-002: PLC スタンバイ", "待機状態")},
    "D2":  {1: ("info", "I-001: 生産完了", "マットレス1枚生産終了")},
    "D100": {
        1: ("warning", "W-001: 全ユニット共通エラー", "ユニット全体の確認が必要"),
    },
    "D900": {
        1:  ("error", "E-001: 非常停止ボタン作動", "装置が停止しました"),
        99: ("error", "E-099: その他PLCからの致命的エラー", "PLCシステムの異常停止"),
    },
}

# =========================
# 監視ロジック（修正：0解除を無視）
# =========================
def monitor_words(plc, prev_state: dict):
    for reg in D_DECODE.keys():
        try:
            value = read_d_word(plc, reg)
            prev = prev_state.get(reg, None)

            if prev is None:
                prev_state[reg] = value
                continue

            if value != prev:
                # 0に戻った時は通知せず状態更新のみ
                if value == 0:
                    prev_state[reg] = value
                    continue

                # ON (値が入った) 時のみ処理
                decode = D_DECODE.get(reg, {})
                if value in decode:
                    log_type, title, msg = decode[value]
                    send_log_to_server(log_type, title, msg)

                    if reg == "D1" and value == 1:
                        call_machine_start()
                    if reg == "D2" and value == 1:
                        call_increment_production(section="spring")
                else:
                    send_log_to_server("info", f"I-{reg}: {reg} 変化", f"{reg}={value}")

                prev_state[reg] = value
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ D読取エラー {reg}: {e}")

def monitor_bits(plc, prev_state: dict, bit_map: dict, prefix: str):
    for dev, (name, desc, level) in bit_map.items():
        try:
            val = int(read_bit(plc, dev))
            prev = prev_state.get(dev, None)

            if prev is None:
                prev_state[dev] = val
                continue

            if val != prev:
                # 1 (ON) になった時だけ通知
                if val == 1:
                    send_log_to_server(level, f"{prefix}-{dev}: {name} ON", desc)
                
                # 0 (OFF) の時は何もしない
                prev_state[dev] = val
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ {prefix}読取エラー {dev}: {e}")

def monitor_plc_once(plc, prev_state: dict):
    monitor_words(plc, prev_state)
    if READ_MODE == "ALL":
        monitor_bits(plc, prev_state, X_MAP, prefix="X")
        monitor_bits(plc, prev_state, Y_MAP, prefix="Y")

# =========================
# メイン
# =========================
def main():
    plc = pymcprotocol.Type3E()
    connected = False
    prev_state = {}

    print(f"🚀 Monitoring Start: {MACHINE_NAME} (No-Off-Log Mode)")

    while True:
        if not connected:
            try:
                plc.connect(PLC_IP, PLC_PORT)
                print(f"✅ PLCに接続しました ({PLC_IP}:{PLC_PORT})")
                connected = True
            except Exception:
                print("⏳ PLC接続リトライ中...")
                time.sleep(5)
                continue

        try:
            monitor_plc_once(plc, prev_state)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❗ 通信切断: {e}")
            connected = False
            try: plc.close()
            except: pass

        time.sleep(POLL_INTERVAL_SEC)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
</file>

<file path="g3-webapp-back-2025/src/app/api/inspections/sections/[section]/dates/route.js">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import pool from '@/lib/db';

/**
 * 日時一覧取得 (GET)
 * 修正内容: ロットごとの最新ショット日から、重複のない「日付リスト」を取得します。
 */
export async function GET(request, { params }) {
  try {
    const { section } = await params;

    if (!section) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { message: 'セクションが指定されていません。' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // 修正ポイント: 
    // 1. フォーマットを 'YYYY-MM-DD' に変更
    // 2. DISTINCT を使い、1日に複数ロットあっても日付が重複しないようにする
    const datesQuery = `
      SELECT DISTINCT
          to_char(MAX(s.captured_at) AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Tokyo', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS inspection_date
      FROM
          inspection_lots l
      JOIN 
          inspection_camera_shots s ON l.id = s.lot_id_ref
      WHERE
          l.section_code = $1
      GROUP BY
          l.id
      ORDER BY
          inspection_date DESC;
    `;

    const result = await pool.query(datesQuery, [section]);
    
    // row.inspection_date の中身は "2026-01-27" のような形式になります
    const dates = result.rows.map(row => row.inspection_date);

    const responseData = {
      section: section,
      dates: dates,
    };

    return NextResponse.json(responseData);

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('DBエラー発生！:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { message: 'サーバーでエラーが発生しました。' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

<file path="g3-webapp-back-2025/src/app/api/inspections/lots/route.js">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import pool from '@/lib/db';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const section = searchParams.get('section');
    const dateParam = searchParams.get('date');
    const page = parseInt(searchParams.get('page') || '1', 10);
    const limit = parseInt(searchParams.get('limit') || '50', 10);
    const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

    // A. DBセッションを日本時間に設定
    await pool.query("SET TIME ZONE 'Asia/Tokyo'");

    // B. 対象日付の決定 (画像がある最新の日付を探す)
    let targetDate = dateParam;
    if (!targetDate) {
      const latestResult = await pool.query(`
        SELECT to_char(s.captured_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') as latest_date
        FROM inspection_camera_shots s
        INNER JOIN inspection_lots l ON s.lot_id_ref = l.id
        ${section ? 'WHERE l.section_code = $1' : ''}
        ORDER BY s.captured_at DESC LIMIT 1
      `, section ? [section] : []);

      targetDate = latestResult.rows[0]?.latest_date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('sv-SE');
    }

    // C. 日付範囲の構築
    const startOfDay = `${targetDate} 00:00:00`;
    const endOfDay = `${targetDate} 23:59:59`;

    // 【核心の修正】ロットの日付ではなく、紐づく「ショット（画像）」の日付で絞り込む
    let whereClause = `
      WHERE EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM inspection_camera_shots s 
        WHERE s.lot_id_ref = inspection_lots.id 
        AND s.captured_at BETWEEN $1 AND $2
      )
    `;
    const queryParams = [startOfDay, endOfDay];

    if (section) {
      whereClause += ` AND section_code = $3`;
      queryParams.push(section);
    }

    // D. データの取得
    const countResult = await pool.query(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM inspection_lots ${whereClause}`, queryParams);
    const totalCount = parseInt(countResult.rows[0].total, 10);
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalCount / limit);

    const lotsResult = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, lot_id, section_code, captured_at
      FROM inspection_lots
      ${whereClause}
      ORDER BY captured_at DESC
      LIMIT $${queryParams.length + 1} OFFSET $${queryParams.length + 2}
    `, [...queryParams, limit, offset]);

    const lots = lotsResult.rows;

    if (lots.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ total_pages: totalPages, current_page: page, lots: [] });
    }

    // E. ショット詳細の取得とレスポンス構築
    const lotIds = lots.map(l => l.id);
    const shotsQuery = `
      SELECT lot_id_ref, camera_id, c4k_seq, status, image_path, id, captured_at
      FROM inspection_camera_shots
      WHERE lot_id_ref = ANY($1::bigint[])
      ORDER BY id ASC;
    `;
    const shotsResult = await pool.query(shotsQuery, [lotIds]);
    const allShots = shotsResult.rows;

    const responseLots = lots.map(lot => {
      const lotShots = allShots.filter(s => s.lot_id_ref === lot.id);
      let representativeImage = null;
      let displayCapturedAt = lot.captured_at;

      if (lotShots.length > 0) {
        // カードに表示する日付はショットの時刻を優先
        displayCapturedAt = lotShots[0].captured_at || lot.captured_at;
        representativeImage = lotShots[0].image_path?.replace('beforeTest', 'afterTest');
      }

      let pass = true;
      let details = {};
      if (lot.section_code === 'alayer') {
        const seqMap = new Map();
        lotShots.forEach(shot => {
          const k = shot.c4k_seq; if (!k) return;
          if (shot.status === 'FAIL') { seqMap.set(k, 'FAIL'); pass = false; }
          else if (shot.status === 'MISSING' && seqMap.get(k) !== 'FAIL') seqMap.set(k, 'MISSING');
          else if (!seqMap.has(k)) seqMap.set(k, 'PASS');
        });
        details.four_k_sequences = Array.from(seqMap.entries()).map(([k, v]) => ({ "c4k_seq": k, "status": v }));
      } else {
        const camMap = new Map();
        lotShots.forEach(shot => {
          const c = shot.camera_id;
          if (shot.status === 'FAIL') { camMap.set(c, 'FAIL'); pass = false; }
          else if (shot.status === 'MISSING' && camMap.get(c) !== 'FAIL') camMap.set(c, 'MISSING');
          else if (!camMap.has(c)) camMap.set(c, 'PASS');
        });
        details.cameras = Array.from(camMap.entries()).map(([k, v]) => ({ "camera_id": k, "status": v }));
      }

      return {
        lot_id: lot.lot_id,
        section: lot.section_code,
        captured_at: displayCapturedAt,
        pass: pass,
        representative_image: representativeImage,
        ...details
      };
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ total_pages: totalPages, current_page: page, lots: responseLots });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('API Error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
